Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The chromatography operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 733. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 660 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on chromatography structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 733. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 660. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a chromatography algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 46. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 535/4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 46 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the chromatography structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 535. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
